This paper introduces a few-shot meta-learning system for regression tasks, using basis functions to reduce complexity and enable accurate function approximation with few samples, outperforming existing methods.

1466
This paper introduces CoDAIL, a decentralized adversarial imitation learning algorithm that models correlated policies in multi-agent systems, enabling better regeneration of complex interactions and outperforming existing methods.

The MILE framework enables large-scale graph embedding by coarsening graphs and refining embeddings with a neural network, significantly improving speed and quality for node classification on massive graphs.

1087
This paper introduces CMOW, a model that captures word order better than CBOW, and proposes a hybrid CBOW-CMOW model that improves linguistic encoding by 8% and performs better on 8 out of 11 downstream tasks.

2224
This paper introduces a new PAC-Bayesian generalization bound for Bayesian neural networks using the negative log-likelihood loss and the Herbst Argument to analyze their risk.

2114
This paper explores using importance sampling for causal reasoning, especially counterfactual inference, within probabilistic programming. It introduces MultiVerse, a prototype engine for approximate causal reasoning, and compares it with Pyro.
